# List of aviation shootdowns and accidents during the Russo-Ukrainian war

This is a **list of Ukrainian, Russian and Russian-separatist aircraft losses during the Russo-Ukrainian War** based on visual evidence or official confirmation from involved parties. It includes proven [helicopter](8286923.md), [fixed-wing aircraft](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fixed-wing_aircraft) and [combat drone](337755.md) (UCAVs) losses from the [War in Donbas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/War_in_Donbas_\(2014%E2%80%932022\)), the current [Russian invasion of Ukraine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine) and the [Wagner Group mutiny](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wagner_Group_mutiny).

## Donbas (2014–2022)

During the War in Donbas, on 20 November 2014, Ukrainian sources reported at a press conference in London, United Kingdom, that their total aerial losses during the conflict in the east were: one Su-24, six Su-25s, two MiG-29s, one An-26, one An-30 and one Il-76.[^cite_note-1][^cite_note-2][^cite_note-3] Another Su-24 was damaged. Helicopter losses amounted to seven Mi-8/17s and five Mi-24s.[^cite_note-TSN1-4][^cite_note-ASN_Wikibase_Occurrence_#_169236-5][^cite_note-ill|IPress.ua|uk-6]

### Ukraine

| Operator | Date | Comments |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 6 June 2014 | An [An-30B](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antonov_An-30) surveillance plane, bort number *Yellow 80*, was shot down with two [shoulder-launched missiles](1389714.md) fired from Pro-Russian rebels during the [siege of Sloviansk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siege_of_Sloviansk). Five crewmen were killed and three survived.[^cite_note-7][^cite_note-8] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 14 June 2014 | An [Ilyushin Il-76](229943.md) transport plane was [shot down](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014_Ukrainian_Air_Force_Il-76_shootdown) as it approached an airport in [Luhansk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luhansk), killing nine crew members and 40 troops on board.[^cite_note-9] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 2 July 2014 | A Ukrainian Sukhoi Su-25, call sign *Blue 06*, crashed due to a technical fault while landing at [Dnipropetrovsk International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dnipropetrovsk_International_Airport). The pilot ejected safely.[^cite_note-10][^cite_note-11] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 2 July 2014 | A Ukrainian Sukhoi Su-24, call sign *Yellow 11*, was damaged by MANPAD fire, the engine caught fire but managed to land but caught fire again and the fire was extinguished. The crew was alive and well.[^cite_note-12] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 14 July 2014 | An [Antonov An-26](908173.md) transport aircraft was shot down by a surface-to-air missile over eastern Ukraine while flying at 6,500 metres (21,300 ft). The Ukrainian defence minister claimed that the altitude is far from the reach of a shoulder-launched missile, suggesting that the aircraft was downed.[^cite_note-13][^cite_note-14][^cite_note-BBC28309034-15] Two crewmembers were captured by pro-Russian militia, four were rescued by Ukrainian forces.[^cite_note-16] The remaining two aviators were found dead on 17 July.[^cite_note-17] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(2)** | 16 July 2014 | An Su-25, call sign *Blue 03*, was shot down over eastern Ukraine near [Amvrosiivka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amvrosiivka), and the pilot ejected successfully. [National Security Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Security_and_Defense_Council_of_Ukraine) spokesman Andriy Lysenko said that it was shot down by a missile fired from a Russian [MiG-29](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MiG-29).[^cite_note-18][^cite_note-19][^cite_note-20] Another Su-25 call sign *Blue 41* was damaged by separatist a MANPAD, the attack fighter landed safety.[^cite_note-21] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(2)** | 23 July 2014 | Two Su-25s, call signs *Blue 04* and *Blue 33*, were shot down in the rebel-held area of [Savur-Mohyla](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Savur-Mohyla). Ukrainian authorities claimed that they were hit by anti-aircraft missiles launched from Russia.[^cite_note-22][^cite_note-23][^cite_note-24][^cite_note-25] Ukrainian Prime Minister [Arseniy Yatsenyuk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arseniy_Yatsenyuk) said that one of the attack planes was probably shot down by an air-to-air missile.[^cite_note-26] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 7 August 2014 | Pro-Russian forces shot down a [MiG-29](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MiG-29) with a [9K37 Buk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buk_missile_system) missile near [Yenakiieve](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yenakiieve). The pilot safely ejected.[^cite_note-27] Separatist sources claimed he was captured and interrogated.[^cite_note-28] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 17 August 2014 | A MiG-29 was shot down by rebels in Luhansk Oblast returning after a successful mission. The pilot ejected and was found by search and rescue.[^cite_note-29] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 20 August 2014 | An Su-24M, *White 27*, was shot down by rebel forces in Luhansk Oblast. The two crew members ejected successfully.[^cite_note-30] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 29 August 2014 | A Sukhoi Su-25, call sign *Blue 08*, was shot down near [Starobesheve](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Starobesheve) by a surface-to-air missile during the [battle of Ilovaisk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_Ilovaisk). The pilot, Captain Vladyslav Voloshyn, ejected and after 4 days reached Ukrainian-controlled territory.[^cite_note-31][^cite_note-32][^cite_note-33] |

| Operator | Date | Comments |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 25 April 2014 | A [Mil Mi-8](957233.md)MT, call sign *Yellow 55*, was destroyed after it was struck by small arms fire on the fuel tanks while preparing for departure in [Kramatorsk Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kramatorsk_Airport).[^cite_note-34] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md)**(2)** | 2 May 2014 | Two [Mil Mi-24s](384591.md) were shot down during large-scale operations during the [Siege of Sloviansk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siege_of_Sloviansk).[^cite_note-35] Rebels claimed they had taken one pilot captive.[^cite_note-36] The [Ukrainian Ministry of Internal Affairs](1400321.md) reported that two helicopters had been shot down, killing two airmen.[^cite_note-37] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 5 May 2014 | A Mil Mi-24 crashed after it was shot down by a heavy machine gun operated by rebel forces. The damaged helicopter landed in a river, and all crew members survived. Later, it was destroyed by an airstrike.[^cite_note-38][^cite_note-39] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 29 May 2014 | A Mil Mi-8 was shot down by rebel forces between Kramatorsk and Mount Karachun, killing 14 on board, among them General [Serhiy Kulchytsky](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Serhiy_Kulchytsky). Another soldier was seriously injured.[^cite_note-40] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 4 June 2014 | Two Mil Mi-24s were forced to land by rebel fire; one was burned out by the damage and could not be repaired.[^cite_note-41] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 21 June 2014 | A Mil Mi-8 crashed while delivering military hardware near [Chuhuiv](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chuhuiv).[^cite_note-42] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 24 June 2014 | The Ukrainian military said that an Mil Mi-8 had been shot down by pro-Russian rebels in the east near Sloviansk, killing all nine people on board.[^cite_note-43] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 7 August 2014 | Pro-Russian forces shot down an Mi-8MT, call sign *Yellow 62*, in Manuilivka, Donetsk Oblast.[^cite_note-44][^cite_note-TSN1-4] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 19 August 2014 | A Mil Mi-8 was shot down near the village of [Heorhiivka, Luhansk Oblast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heorhiivka,_Luhansk_Oblast).[^cite_note-ill\|IPress.ua\|uk-6] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 20 August 2014 | A Mil Mi-24 was shot down near the town of [Horlivka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Horlivka) according to the [Ukrainian Ministry of Defence](14284651.md). Both pilots died in the attack.[^cite_note-45] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 27 August 2014 | A Mil Mi-8, call sign *Yellow 59*, crashed while landing in Olenivka, Donetsk Oblast.[^cite_note-ASN_Wikibase_Occurrence_#_169236-5] |

### Russia

| [Russia](25391.md) Russian Army | October 2016 | A Forpost drone was lost in eastern Ukraine.[^cite_note-mediumforpost-46] |
| [Russia](25391.md) Russian Army | October 2016 | A Forpost drone was lost in eastern Ukraine.[^cite_note-mediumforpost-46] |

## Russian invasion of Ukraine (2022–present)

### Ukraine

#### Fixed-wing aircraft

##### 2022

| Service | Date | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 24 February | Unknown | Ukrainian officials reported that a military aircraft of unspecified type was destroyed by Russian missiles in Odesa Air Base.[^cite_note-47] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 24 February | [L-39M1 Albatros](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aero_L-39_Albatros) | An L-39M1 Albatros *Blue 106* light attack jet from the [39th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/39th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade_\(Ukraine\)) was shot down by Russian aircraft in [Khmelnytskyi Oblast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khmelnytskyi_Oblast). Pilot Dmytro Kolomiyets was killed.[^cite_note-48][^cite_note-2022RUWAir-49] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(5)** | 24 February | 5 [MiG-29](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MiG-29) | Five MiG-29s from [40th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/40th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) were shot down in air combat by Russian aircraft:  MiG-29MU1, *White 05*, piloted by Lt. Colonel [Yerko Viacheslav Volodymyrovych](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yerko_Viacheslav_Volodymyrovych?action=edit\&redlink=1) was shot down in Volodymyrivka, the pilot died.[^cite_note-50][^cite_note-51] Another was shot down over Dnipro river near [Kosyn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kosyn) in Kyiv Oblast. The pilot ejected.[^cite_note-52][^cite_note-aircrafttype-53] One MiG-29 was shot down in Sosnivka, Vyshhorod Raion, Kyiv Oblast, the pilot ejected.[^cite_note-54]  One MiG-29UB with Volodomyr Kokhanskyi and Roman Pasulko was shot down over Sukholisy, Bila Tserkva Raion.[^cite_note-55][^cite_note-56][^cite_note-57][^cite_note-58] Finally MiG-29MU1, *Blue 03*, piloted by [Viacheslav Radionov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Viacheslav_Radionov) was shot down near Mala Soltanivka, Kyiv Oblast.[^cite_note-59] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(4)** | 24 February | 2 [Su-25](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sukhoi_Su-25) 2 L-39 Albatros | Russian missile strikes against [Kulbakino Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kulbakino_Air_Base) destroyed two stored [Su-25](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sukhoi_Su-25) attack aircraft; *Blue 12*, *Blue 42* and two [L-39](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aero_L-39_Albatros) trainers.[^cite_note-60][^cite_note-61][^cite_note-62] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(7)** | 24 February | 6 L-39 Albatros 1 MiG-29 | Russian missile strikes against [Chuhuiv Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chuhuiv_air_base_attack) destroyed six L-39 trainers in non-operational storage. An operational MiG-29 was damaged beyond repair after crashing during take-off.[^cite_note-63][^cite_note-64] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(5)** | 24 February | 4 MiG-29 1 Su-24 | Russian missile strikes against [Starokostiantyniv Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Starokostiantyniv_Air_Base) destroyed at least four [MiG-29s](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MiG-29) and at least one [Su-24](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-24) in non-operational storage.[^cite_note-65] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 24 February | [An-26](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/An-26) | An An-26 transport aircraft, call sign *Blue 59*, was shot down south of Kyiv; five from a crew of fourteen were killed.[^cite_note-66][^cite_note-67] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(2)** | 24 February | 2 [Su-27](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-27) | Russian strikes left a [Su-27](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-27), codename *Blue 37* destroyed and caused damage to another Su-27 on [Ozerne Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ozerne_Air_Base) in [Zhytomyr Oblast](1567596.md).[^cite_note-68][^cite_note-69] Seven pilots and servicemen, including Lt. Colonel [Eduard Vahorovskyi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eduard_Vahorovskyi) were killed.[^cite_note-70][^cite_note-71] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 24 February | [Mig-29](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mig-29) | Russian strikes damaged a Mig-29 parked in the ground in [Vasylkiv Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vasylkiv_Air_Base). The aircraft was later repaired.[^cite_note-72] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 27 February | MiG-29 | Russian strikes on [Lutsk Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lutsk_Air_Base), destroyed a MiG-29, call sign *Blue 28* that was stored in a hangar.[^cite_note-73] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 24 February | [Su-24M](382335.md) | An Su-24M, call sign *White 20* was shot down by Russian forces in [Hostomel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hostomel); both pilots, Major Dmitry Kulikov and Major Nikolai Savchuk were killed.[^cite_note-militarizm.su-74][^cite_note-unaspotalva-75][^cite_note-76] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 24 February | [Il-76MD](229943.md) | An Il-76MD, registration *UR-76697* of the [25th Transport Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/25th_Transport_Aviation_Brigade) was destroyed by a Russian strike at [Melitopol Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Melitopol_Air_Base).[^cite_note-77] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(5)** | 24–26 February | 5 Su-25 | Five Su-25s from the [299th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/299th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) were lost in Kherson Oblast: On 24 February, Su-25M1, call sign *Blue 19*, was shot down near [Chaplynka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chaplynka), pilot Lt. Col [Oleksandr Zhybrov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oleksandr_Zhybrov?action=edit\&redlink=1) ejected but died.[^cite_note-78]  Su-25M1 call sign *Blue 30*, was shot down in the same event, the pilot [Oleksander Scherbakov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oleksander_Scherbakov?action=edit\&redlink=1) was killed.[^cite_note-79][^cite_note-80] The same day a Su-25, call sign *Blue 17* flown by Mayor Rostislav Lazarenko was damaged on Chorna Dolyna, Lazarenko managed to land the aircraft on Melitopol but the airframe was damaged beyond repair and was further damaged by strafing Russian aircraft.[^cite_note-81][^cite_note-82] On 26 February, Su-25M1K, call sign *Blue 31*, from the 299th TAB was shot down by a Russian AD near [Fedorivka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fedorivka).[^cite_note-83] Captain Andriy Antikhovich died.[^cite_note-84][^cite_note-85] Finally Su-25, call sign *Blue 49* was shot down by Russian forces using a [9K333 Verba](47083707.md) MANPAD in Molodizhne, Kherson Oblast, pilot Andriy Maksymov ejected and was captured on 26–27 February.[^cite_note-86][^cite_note-87] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 25 February | [MiG-29](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MiG-29) | A MiG-29, *Blue 35*, was lost between Horolivka and Hrushka in [Ivano-Frankivsk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ivano-Frankivsk).[^cite_note-88] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 25 February | Su-27P | A Su-27P, call sign *Blue 100* was shot down over Kyiv by Russian [S-400](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S-400) SAMs; the crash damaged a residential complex. The pilot, Colonel [Oleksandr Oksanchenko](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oleksandr_Oksanchenko) was killed.[^cite_note-\:0-89][^cite_note-90] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 25 February | Su-25M1K | An Su-25M1K, call sign *Blue 39*, piloted by [Hennadii Matuliak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hennadii_Matuliak), was shot down by a Russian jet-fighter near [Hlibivka, Vyshhorod](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vyshhorod), Kyiv Oblast.[^cite_note-91][^cite_note-92] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 25 February | Su-24M | Su-24M, call sign *White 08*, was shot down in Voznesenske, Zolotonosha Raion, Cherkasy Oblast.[^cite_note-93] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(6)** | 26 February | 6 MiG-29 | A Russian missile attack on [Ivano-Frankivsk International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ivano-Frankivsk_International_Airport) destroyed six MiG-29s[^cite_note-94] and killed one Ukrainian servicemen.[^cite_note-95] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 26 February | Su-25 | A Su-25, in grey camouflage painting was destroyed in the ground at Kulbakino Air Base, Mykolaiv Oblast.[^cite_note-96] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(4)** | 27 February | 4 Su-24 | Military aircraft were destroyed by Russian attacks on [Lutsk Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lutsk_Air_Base), Commercial satellite imagery showed the wreckage of four stored Su-24s.[^cite_note-forbes1-97][^cite_note-98] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 27 February | Su-24M | An Su-24M, call sign *White 77*, from the [7th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/7th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) was shot down near [Bucha, Kyiv Oblast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bucha,_Kyiv_Oblast). The pilots, Commander [Ruslan Bilous](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ruslan_Bilous?action=edit\&redlink=1) and Commander [Roman Dovhaliuk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_Dovhaliuk?action=edit\&redlink=1) were killed and awarded the [Order of Bohdan Khmelnytsky](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_Bohdan_Khmelnytsky).[^cite_note-99][^cite_note-100][^cite_note-unaspotalva-75] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 27 February | An-26 | An An-26 in storage at [Kherson International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kherson_International_Airport) was destroyed following the Russian capture of the airport.[^cite_note-101] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 28 February | Su-27S | An Su-27S, call sign *Blue 11* was shot down by MANPADS over [Kropyvnytskyi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kropyvnytskyi), [Kirovohrad Oblast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kirovohrad_Oblast). Its pilot, Maj. [Stepan Chobanu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stepan_Chobanu?action=edit\&redlink=1), was killed.[^cite_note-ZelesnkyAwards-102][^cite_note-103] He was awarded Hero of Ukraine on 3 March.[^cite_note-104] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 1 March | Su-24M | An Su-24M, from the [7th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/7th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) was shot down near the village of Krasenyvka, [Zolotonosha](5516318.md), Cherkasy Oblast. The pilots were wounded, but survived.[^cite_note-105] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 2 March | Su-25M1 | An Su-25M1, *Blue 29* from [299th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/299th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) piloted by [Oleksandr Korpan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oleksandr_Korpan) was lost over [Starokostiantyniv](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Starokostiantyniv), Khmelnytskyi Oblast. Korpan died as a result.[^cite_note-106][^cite_note-107][^cite_note-108] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 2 March | MiG-29 | A MiG-29 piloted by [Oleksandr Brynzhala](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oleksandr_Brynzhala?action=edit\&redlink=1) was shot down by Russian aircraft over Kyiv.[^cite_note-109][^cite_note-110] On 2 April, Brynzhala was awarded [Hero of Ukraine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hero_of_Ukraine) order, posthumously.[^cite_note-111] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 2 March | Su-24M | An Su-24M, *White 66*, was shot down near [Novohrad-Volynskyi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Novohrad-Volynskyi); the pilot, Colonel [Mykola Kovalenko](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mykola_Kovalenko?action=edit\&redlink=1), and navigator, Captain [Yevhen Kazimirov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yevhen_Kazimirov?action=edit\&redlink=1), died.[^cite_note-112][^cite_note-militarizm.su-74][^cite_note-unaspotalva-75] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 3 March | Su-25M1 | An Su-25M1, call sign *Blue 40*, from the 299th Tactical Aviation Brigade piloted by Captain [Vadym Moroz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vadym_Moroz?action=edit\&redlink=1) was lost over Mykolaiv.[^cite_note-113][^cite_note-114] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(2)** | 4 March | Su-25/UB L-39 Albatros | An Su-25 or UB variant, was destroyed in the ground by Russian forces in Kulbakino Air Base, Mykolaiv Oblast.[^cite_note-115] Also a L-39M1 Albatros trainer was destroyed as well.[^cite_note-116] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 5 March | Unknown | An unidentified Ukrainian jetfighter was shot down by Russian aircraft over Zhytomyr Oblast. The pilot ejected and survived.[^cite_note-117] |

| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 6 March | An-26 | An An-26 cargo plane, call sign *Blue 57*, was destroyed at [Vinnytsia Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Havryshivka_Vinnytsia_International_Airport) after [a Russian missile attack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vinnytsia_strikes_\(2022%E2%80%93present\)).[^cite_note-NYT1-119] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 6 March | Su-25 | An Su-25 was lost in Snihurivka, Mykolaiv Oblast according to the Ukrainian Mayor of Mykolaiv and Russian sources that reported the shotdown of a Ukrainian Su-25 by a Russian Su-35.[^cite_note-120] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(2)** | 8 March | 2 MiG-29 | Two MiG-29 were shot down by Russian forces in Zhytomyr highway. From one of the shotdown fighters the pilot ejected and survived,[^cite_note-121] but from the other the pilot Major Andriy Liutashyn was killed.[^cite_note-122][^cite_note-123] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 9 March | MiG-29 | A MiG-29, *White 10*, piloted by Commander Yevhen Lysenko; his fighter was destroyed by Russian air defenses while fighting Russian aircraft near [Zhytomyr](1025103.md).[^cite_note-124][^cite_note-125] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 9 March | Su-24M | An Su-24M, call sign *White 26*, was shot down by a Russian missiles near [Lebedyn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lebedyn), Sumy Oblast, the pilots bailed out and survived.[^cite_note-126] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 10 March | Su-27 | At least one Su-27 was damaged or destroyed during a Russian missile strike on [Kanatove Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kanatove_\(air_base\)) confirmed by satellite imagery.[^cite_note-127] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 12 March | Su-24M | An Su-24M was shot down by Russian forces near Bilyaivka village, [Beryslav Raion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beryslav_Raion), Kherson Oblast. The pilots, Valeriy Oshkalo and Roman Chekhun, died.[^cite_note-128][^cite_note-unaspotalva-75] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 13 March | MiG-29 | Ukrainian military sources reported that a MiG-29 piloted by Cpt. [Stepan Tarabalka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stepan_Tarabalka) was lost while defending Ukrainian airspace against Russian jetfighters.[^cite_note-129][^cite_note-130] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 14 March | Su-25 | An Su-25 was shot down by Russian forces in [Volnovakha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Volnovakha), Donetsk Oblast. The pilot, Roman Vasyliuk, captured, was later released on 24 April in a prisoner swap.[^cite_note-131][^cite_note-132] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 15 March | MiG-29MU1 | A MiG-29MU1 *White 15* was shot down in Nova [Nova Basan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nova_Basan), Chernihiv Oblast. Journalists from *[France 24](1263244.md)* recorded the ordnance next to the craft being destroyed by the Ukrainian military.[^cite_note-133][^cite_note-134] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 18 March | Unknown | A Russian missile strike on the Lviv State Aircraft Plant designed for MiG-29 repairs, which also contained foreign aircraft, left an unknown number of aircraft destroyed and damaged.[^cite_note-Lvivhangar-135] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 21 March | Su-24 | An Su-24 piloted by Viacheslav Khodakivskyi was lost near Pokrovsky district, [Zaporizhzhia](1873579.md). The pilot died.[^cite_note-136] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 22 March | Su-24 | An Su-24 bomber, call sign *White 49* was shot down below Russian lines in [Izium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Izium); the pilot Oleksiy Kovalenko was killed, the navigator Serhiy Verbytsky was wounded but evaded capture.[^cite_note-137][^cite_note-138][^cite_note-139] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 23 March | MiG-29 | A MiG-29 piloted by Commander Dmytro Chumachenko from the [204th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/204th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) was lost in air to air combat by Russian aircraft in Zhytomyr Oblast.[^cite_note-140][^cite_note-141][^cite_note-142] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 30 March | Su-24M | An Su-24M bomber, *Blue 41*, was shot down in Kirovohrad. The crew from 7th Tactical Aviation Brigade, Maksym Sikalenko, and navigator Kostiantin Horodnychev, were killed.[^cite_note-143] A Su-24 was recorded damaged with a trail of smoke in Rivne, western Ukraine on social media.[^cite_note-forbes30may-144][^cite_note-unaspotalva-75] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(2)** | 4 April | 2 [Il-76MD](229943.md) | Two Il-76MD from the [25th Transport Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/25th_Transport_Aviation_Brigade) were destroyed on the ground by Russian forces at [Melitopol Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Melitopol_Air_Base). One of the cargo planes destroyed was in operational state and the other undergoing repairs.[^cite_note-145] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 10 April | Su-25 | An Su-25 was destroyed in the ground inside its hangar at Dnipro Airbase by Russian rocket attacks, a technician was wounded as well.[^cite_note-146] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 15 April | Su-25 | An Su-25 was reported shot down by Russian forces in Izium;[^cite_note-147] the downing of the fighter was recorded by a military IR camera.[^cite_note-148] The pilot, Captain Yehor Serediuk, was reported killed near Izium on 15 April.[^cite_note-149][^cite_note-150] Serediuk was awarded Hero of Ukraine.[^cite_note-151] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 30 April | Su-24M | An Su-24M, *White 87*, was damaged by a Russian missile in Kharkiv and crashed near Nova Praha, Kirovohrad Oblast. The crew survived.[^cite_note-152] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 6 May | Su-24M | An Su-24M, call sign *White 12*, crashed by a pilot error in Voznesensk airbase, Kherson. Both pilots survived.[^cite_note-USU24L-153][^cite_note-154][^cite_note-155] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 14 May | Su-25 | An Su-25, callsign *Blue 45*, from the [299th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/299th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade), piloted by Captain Serhiy Parkhomenko, was shot down in [Huliaipole](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Huliaipole), [Zaporizhzhia Oblast](494936.md).[^cite_note-156][^cite_note-157] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 19 May | Su-24 | An Su-24, call sign *White 69* from the [7th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/7th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) was lost near [Soledar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soledar) during a combat mission.[^cite_note-158] Pilots Lt. Colonel Ihor Khmara and navigator Mayor Illia Nehar died.[^cite_note-159][^cite_note-160] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 5 June | Su-27P1M | An Su-27P1M, call sign *Blue 38*, was shot down while flying at low altitude near [Orikhiv](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orikhiv), Zaporizhzhia Oblast. The pilot, former Commander of the [831st Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/831st_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) Lt. Col [Dmytro Fisher](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dmytro_Fisher) died.[^cite_note-161][^cite_note-Su27Ukrainian-162] The aircraft was reportedly destroyed by the debris of a Russian cruise missile or a large drone.[^cite_note-163][^cite_note-164] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(2)** | 16 June | MiG-29, Su-24 | Two aircraft, a MiG-29 and a Su-24 bomber were destroyed on the ground in [Voznesensk airbase](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Voznesensk_\(air_base\)), [Mykolaiv Oblast](494928.md), after a Russian attack.[^cite_note-165][^cite_note-17Jro-166][^cite_note-167] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 26 June | Su-24MR | An Su-24MR, call sign *White 84* was lost during a military mission on [Snake Island](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snake_Island_\(Ukraine\)) in the Black Sea. Pilot Commander of the [40th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/40th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade) Colonel Mykhailo Matiushenko died and navigator [Major](201920.md) Yuriy Krasylnikov was missing.[^cite_note-168][^cite_note-169][^cite_note-170] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | before June | Su-24MR | An Su-24MR, was shot down during a bombing mission in Snake Island, both crewmen; Captain Victor Zhos and its navigator Sergei Pliguzov ejected and survived.[^cite_note-171] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 1 July | Su-27 | An Su-27 was recorded damaged being transported for repairs.[^cite_note-172] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(4)** | 24 July | 3 Su-27, 1 Su-24 | Three Su-27s and one Su-24 were destroyed during a Russian missile strike on [Kanatove Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kanatove_\(air_base\)), one official died and 17 other servicemen were injured.[^cite_note-173][^cite_note-174][^cite_note-175] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 26 July | Su-25M1 | The Chief of Intelligence of the [299th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/299th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade), Oleksandr Kukurba, died while flying a combat mission on his [Su-25](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-25)M1, *Blue 16* in Dnipro.[^cite_note-176][^cite_note-177][^cite_note-2022RUWAir-49] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 7 August | Il-76MD | An Il-76MD, with registration *UR-76699*, was recorded under Russian control in [Melitopol Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Melitopol_Air_Base).[^cite_note-178] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 21 August | Su-27S | An Su-27S *Blue 26* piloted by Lt. Col Pavlo Babych was lost during a combat mission. The pilot died.[^cite_note-179][^cite_note-180] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 7 September | Su-25 | Ukrainian officials reported the death of 299th Tactical Aviation Brigade pilot Vadym Blahovisnyi while flying a combat mission on an [Su-25](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-25).[^cite_note-181][^cite_note-182] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 19 September | Su-25 | The remains of an Su-25 were found in Yehorivka, Donetsk Oblast. The aircraft was destroyed by a Russian [9K33 Osa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/9K33_Osa).[^cite_note-183][^cite_note-184][^cite_note-185] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 25 September | MiG-29 | A MiG-29 piloted by Major Taras Viktorovych Redkin was reported lost by Russian aircraft during a [AGM-88 HARM](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AGM-88_HARM) mission.[^cite_note-186][^cite_note-187] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 29 September | Su-24M | An Su-24M was shot down by Russian surface-to-air missile in Kaluga, Kherson Oblast. The aircraft was recorded on video with one of its engines on fire before it crashed. The pilots Lt. Col Yevhen Soloviov and Captain Artem Khanko ejected.[^cite_note-188][^cite_note-189][^cite_note-USU24L-153] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) **(2)** | 10–11 October | Su-27P1M, Su-24M | Two fighter jets were lost over Shishatsk, [Poltava Oblast](1567650.md). One Su-27P1M, call sign *Blue 58* from [39th Tactical Aviation Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/39th_Tactical_Aviation_Brigade_\(Ukraine\)) piloted by Colonel Oleg Shupik was lost, Shupik died.[^cite_note-190][^cite_note-191] Another aircraft, a Su-24M, *Yellow 59*, was shot down by an enemy missile after returning from a mission on 12 October.[^cite_note-192][^cite_note-193] One of the pilots survived, the other, Dmytro Bortnyk died.[^cite_note-194] Russian media reported on 27 November that an Su-24 was shot down in air combat by a MiG-31 with a missile.[^cite_note-TD10Nov-195] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 12 October | MiG-29 | A MiG-29, call sign *Blue 10*, was lost while intercepting Russian loitering ammunition [Shahed 136](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shahed_136) drones in Vinnytsia. One Shahed-136 detonated near the jet, [shrapnel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shrapnel_\(fragment\)) struck the [cockpit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cockpit) forcing the pilot, Maj. [Vadym Voroshylov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vadym_Voroshylov)[^cite_note-196] to [eject](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ejection_seat).[^cite_note-197][^cite_note-198] |
| **Total** | **106** | 10 L-39, 28 MiG-29, 15 Su-27, 24 Su-24, 20 Su-25, 3 An-26, 4 Il-76, 2 unidentified. |  |

| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 25 September | MiG-29 | A MiG-29 was destroyed at [Kulbakino Air Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kulbakino_Air_Base), Mykolaiv.[^cite_note-223][^cite_note-224] |
| [Ukraine](31750.md) | 10 October | Su-25 | An Su-25 was destroyed by a ZALA Lancet 3 drone attack on the tarmac of Dolgintsevo air base near Krivoi Rog Air Base.[^cite_note-225] |

| [Russia](25391.md) | 14 March | Su-25 | A Su-25, registration number *RF-90965*, 32 Yellow was damaged by enemy fire, likely MANPADS, but managed to return to its base.[^cite_note-567] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 14 March | Su-34 | Registration number RF-95010, tail number *Red 35* was found wrecked.[^cite_note-568][^cite_note-569] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 15 March | Su-30SM | A Su-30SM *RF-81773*, codename *Red 62*, was shot down and crashed in Bryhadyrivka Kharkiv Oblast. Lt. Col Yevgeny Kyslyakov[^cite_note-570] and Capt. Aleksander Pozinich from [14th Guards Fighter Aviation Regiment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kursk_Vostochny_Airport), were killed.[^cite_note-571] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 16 March | Su-25SM | Registration number RF-93027, tail number *Red 12*, of the 18th Attack Aviation Regiment was found in Blystavytsya, Kyiv Oblast. The pilot reportedly ejected.[^cite_note-572] |

Both pilots were recorded bailing out.[^cite_note-575][^cite_note-576][^cite_note-577][^cite_note-578] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 12 May | Su-25 | Pilot Colonel Nicolai Markov was shot down in [Popasna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Popasna), while flying for a Russian [PMC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Private_military_company).[^cite_note-579] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 21 April | Su-34 | Registration number RF-95858, tail number *Red 43* was shot down near Zaporizhzhia.[^cite_note-580] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 18 May | Su-34 | A Su-34 was reported shot down in Kharkiv, the pilot Cmdr. Vladimir Fetisov was reported killed.[^cite_note-581][^cite_note-582] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 22 May | Su-25 | A Su-25 piloted by Major General (R) [Kanamat Botashev](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kanamat_Botashev) was shot down Pylypchatyne, Bakhmut while flying for Wagner [PMC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Private_military_company).[^cite_note-583][^cite_note-584] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 27 May | Su-25SM | A Su-25M with registration number RF-95152, tail number *Blue 15* was shot down near Chornobaivka. Pilot lieutenant Nikita Malkovsky was killed.[^cite_note-585] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 10 June | Su-25SM | Registration number RF-91957, tail number *Red 3* was heavily damaged after being hit by MANPADS over Ukraine. The pilot made an emergency landing.[^cite_note-586] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 17 June | Su-25 | Pilot error crashed registration number RF-91965, tail number *Red-09* in Belgorod.[^cite_note-587] Russian *Fighter-Bomber* [Telegram](41501305.md) channel published a headcam video footage of the pilot ejecting; he lived.[^cite_note-588][^cite_note-589] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 18 June | Su-25 | Pilot Andrei Vladimirovich Fedorchukov was shot down with [9K38 Igla](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/9K38_Igla) MANPADS in [Svitlodarsk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Svitlodarsk). Fedorchukov was working for the [Wagner Group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wagner_Group) and was captured.[^cite_note-590][^cite_note-591] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 21 June | Su-25 | A Su-25, registration number *RF-90958*, 23 Yellow crashed near Mankovo-Kalitvenskoe, Rostov, Russia. The pilot Vladimir Krot died.[^cite_note-592][^cite_note-593] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 3 July | Su-25 | A Su-25, was shot down in the area of Sievierodonetsk. Pilot Captain Anton Mikhailovich Romanov was killed.[^cite_note-594] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 17 July | Su-34 | Registartion number RF-95890, tail number *Red 51* was shot down near [Alchevsk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alchevsk), Luhansk Oblast.[^cite_note-595][^cite_note-596] Possibly [friendly fire](202484.md).[^cite_note-597] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 19 July | Su-35S | Damaged and crashed in the vicinity of Shlyakhove, Beryslav Raion, Kherson Oblast. The pilot ejected. Footage of the wreckage was posted on February 4, 2023.[^cite_note-598] |
| [Russia](25391.md) **(11)** | 9 August | 7 Su-24, 4 Su-30 | An explosion at [Saky Airbase](71495353.md) in [Novofedorivka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Novofedorivka) destroyed five Su-24s, damaged two, destroyed three Su-30s and damaged one.[^cite_note-599][^cite_note-600][^cite_note-601] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 18 August | Su-30SM | Remains of registration number *RF-81771* tail number *Red 60*, were discovered near Mala Komyshuvakha, Izium. The fate of the pilots remained unknown.[^cite_note-602][^cite_note-603] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 22 August | Su-25 | Shot down using [9K38 Igla](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/9K38_Igla) MANPADs near Bakhmut.[^cite_note-604] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 7 September | Su-25SM | A Su-25 of registration number RF-95134, tail number *Blue 04* was shot down by MANPAD fire near Volokhiv Yar, Kharkiv Oblast. The pilot Alexander Arzhanykh survived.[^cite_note-605][^cite_note-606] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 9 September | Su-34 | Remains of Su-34, registration number RF-95004, tail number *Red 20*, were discovered near Izium. The fate of the crew is unknown.[^cite_note-607][^cite_note-608][^cite_note-609] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 11 September | Su-25 | A Su-25 crashed during taking off from [Millerovo air base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Millerovo_air_base), the pilot Danil Dolbik was killed.[^cite_note-610][^cite_note-611] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 11 September | Su-34 | Shot down possibly by friendly fire near Vorontsivka in Crimea. Both crew members ejected safely.[^cite_note-612] |
| [Russia](25391.md) **(2)** | 24 September | Su-34 | Shot down and recorded; one engine caught fire and crashed, allegedly downed by MANPADS, in Hlushchenkove, Kharkiv Oblast. Registration number RF-95005, tail number *Red 22*,[^cite_note-613] was recorded shot down by a missile and crashed in Petropavlivka, [Kupiansk Raion](8613952.md).[^cite_note-614] The crew ejected.[^cite_note-615] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 1 October | [MiG-31](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MiG-31) | Crashed after takeoff on [Belbek military airbase](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sevastopol_International_Airport), [Sevastopol](51587.md). In early November satellite imagery showed the remains of a MiG-31 on the bottom of a cliff after the end of the runway.[^cite_note-TD10Nov-195] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 2 October | Su-34 | Remains of registration number RF-81852, tail number *Red 09*, were discovered near [Lyman](6862925.md).[^cite_note-616] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 6 October | Su-34 | Remains of a Su-34 were recorded at Popivka, Luhansk Oblast.[^cite_note-617] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 9 October | [Su-24](382335.md) | On training mission, crash landed near Sibirki in [Rostov Oblast](478744.md) and caught fire, possibly due to a technical malfunction. Both crew evacuated safely.[^cite_note-618] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 9 October | Su-25 | Crashed in an accident in Rogalik, Rostov Oblast, 30 km from Millerovo airfield.[^cite_note-619] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 17 October | Su-34 | A Su-34, registration number *RF-81726*, 20 Red, [crashed](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Yeysk_military_aircraft_crash) into a residential building in [Yeysk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yeysk) near the [Sea of Azov](78019.md) during a training flight. Engine fire caused by a bird strike.[^cite_note-620][^cite_note-621] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 2 December | Su-24M | A Su-24M, registration number RF-93798, tail number *Blue 48,* operated by Wagner PMC, lost near [Klishchiivka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Klishchiivka), south of Bakhmut. Pilot Alexander Antonov and navigator Vladimir Nikishin were killed.[^cite_note-622][^cite_note-623] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 3 December | Su-34 | Images of a Russian Su-34 were displayed on media, footage of the wrecksite were displayed later on 3 January 2023.[^cite_note-624] |
| [Russia](25391.md) **(2)** | 5 December | [Tu-22M](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tupolev_Tu-22M), Tu-95 | Tu-22M, RF-34110, and a [Tu-95](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tupolev_Tu-95) were damaged by long-range drone attack on the [Dyagilevo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dyagilevo_air_base) and [Engels-2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Engels-2_air_base) air bases.[^cite_note-625][^cite_note-626][^cite_note-627] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 9 December | Su-25SM | Damaged after an emergency gear-up landing at Primorsko-Akhtarsk Air Base in Krasnodar Krai.[^cite_note-628] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | Unknown date 2022 | Su-25SM | The remains of a Su-25SM, Registration number RF-91968, tail number *Blue 09* were recorded and released in 2024.[^cite_note-629] |
| **Total** | **68** | 1 MiG-31, 2 Su-35, 10 Su-30, 24 Su-25, 9 Su-24, 19 Su-34, 1 Tu-22, 1 Tu-95, 1 An-26. |  |

| [Russia](25391.md) | 10 March | Su-27 | Damaged in a partisan attack on [Uglovoye airfield](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uglovoye_\(airfield\)) in [Primorsky Krai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Primorsky_Krai). Video posted on 10 March 2023.[^cite_note-636] |

| [Russia](25391.md) | 7 April | Su-25 | Shot down by airborne anti-aircraft unit near [Oleksandrivka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oleksandrivka). The pilot probably ejected.[^cite_note-638] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 8 May | Su-24 | Set on fire in a partisan attack on [Novosibirsk Aircraft Production Association Plant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Novosibirsk_Aircraft_Production_Association_Plant). The aircraft was in storage and only the landing gear was damaged.[^cite_note-639] |
| [Russia](25391.md) **(2)** | 13 May | Su-34, Su-35S | Both shot down by [Ukrainian Patriot Defence Systems](52024.md) in [Bryansk Oblast](476473.md). The crewmen of the Su-34 Mikhail Chuprakov and Andrey Olennikov died. The pilot of the Su-35 Gleb Polyakov died.[^cite_note-640][^cite_note-reuters05-23-641][^cite_note-642] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 24 June | Il-22M | A Il-22M-11, registration number *RF-75917*, was shot down in [Voronezh Oblast](473401.md) by the Wagner Group during the [Wagner Group rebellion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wagner_Group_rebellion).[^cite_note-643][^cite_note-644] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 29 June | Su-25 | A Su-25 was recorded hit with Igla missile by anti-aircraft unit of [10th Mountain Assault Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/10th_Mountain_Assault_Brigade_\(Ukraine\)).[^cite_note-645][^cite_note-646] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 17 July | [Su-25](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sukhoi_Su-25) | Crashed near [Yeysk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yeysk) in the Sea of Azov.[Southern Military District](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Southern_Military_District) reported that it was conducting a test flight. | [Russia](25391.md) | 19 August | [Tu-22M](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tupolev_Tu-22M) | A Tu-22M was destroyed on the ground of [Soltsy-2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soltsy-2_\(air_base\)) air base in [Novgorod](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Novgorod) by a Ukrainian drone attack.[^cite_note-648] |
| [Russia](25391.md) **(4)** | 29 August | [Il-76](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Il-76) | Four Il-76 were attacked by a Ukrainian drone attack at [Pskov airfield](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pskov_Airport), two were burned out, two were damaged.[^cite_note-649] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 29 September | [Su-35](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-35) | An Su-35 was shot down over [Tokmak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tokmak), possibly by friendly fire. The pilot was killed.[^cite_note-650][^cite_note-651] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 7 November | [Su-24](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-24) | The remains of an Su-24, registration number RF-92025, tail number *Blue 48*, were found in Soledar. Likely lost on 3 December 2022.[^cite_note-652] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 5 December | [Su-24](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-24) | Lost over the [Black Sea](3386.md), Ukraine claimed the aircraft was shot down by a [Patriot Air Defense System](52024.md).[^cite_note-653] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 17 December | [Su-25](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-25) | A Su-25 crashed during unknown circunstances, the pilot Dmitry Moiseev did not survive.[^cite_note-654] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 22 December | [Su-34](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Su-34) | Shot down in southern Ukraine, one confirmed destroyed by Telegram channel Fighterbomber.[^cite_note-655] |

| [Russia](25391.md) | 3 March | Mi-8 | A Russian Mi-8AMTSh helicopter was destroyed amid a Ukrainian drone attack or an friendly fire episode by a Russian jetfighter on Rostov Oblast. The crew of three was killed.[^cite_note-848] |

| [Russia](25391.md) | 20 March | Ka-52 | A Russian [Kamov Ka-52](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kamov_Ka-52) combat helicopter was shot down by a Ukrainian FPV drone in Pokrovsky, Donetsk. The crew of two was killed by FPV drones.[^cite_note-850] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 29 April | Mi-8 and Mi-28 | Ukrainian forces launched FPV drones attacks on Russian helicopter grounded at a forward base in Voronezh Oblast, Russia. One Mi-8 and Mi-28 were damaged.[^cite_note-851] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 4 September | 2 helicopters | Ukrainian forces launched drone attacks on Sochi airbase, Russia. Destroying two helicopters.[^cite_note-852] |
| **Total** | **7** | 2 Mi-8, 1 Mi-28, 1 Ka-27, 1 Ka-52, 2 unidentified |  |

#### Unmanned combat aerial vehicle (UCAV)

##### 2022

| Operator | Date | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 11 March | Forpost | A [Forpost](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IAI_Searcher) drone was shot down in Zhytomyr.[^cite_note-853][^cite_note-TheDrive1-854] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 7 April | Orion - I | An [Orion drone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kronshtadt_Orion) was reported destroyed in Ukraine. The Ukrainian MOD released images of the drone wreck.[^cite_note-855][^cite_note-forbesorion-431] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 25 April | Orion - I | The remains of an Orion UAV was found in Ukraine.[^cite_note-856] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 25 June | Orion - I | An Orion UAV crashed into a tree in the streets of Mariupol.[^cite_note-857] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 4 July | Forpost | A Forpost drone crashed into a house in Taganrog, Rostov Oblast.[^cite_note-858][^cite_note-859] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 21 July | Orion - I | The remains of an Orion drone were found in Ukraine.[^cite_note-860] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 30 July | Forpost | The remains of a Forpost drone, registration number 932, were recorded in Bulgaria.[^cite_note-861] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 23 September | Mohajer-6 | An Iranian-made [Mohajer 6](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qods_Mohajer-6), presumably operated by Russian forces, was brought down by Ukrainian forces in the Black Sea near Odesa.[^cite_note-862] The drone was in relatively intact condition and was captured by the Ukrainian forces.[^cite_note-Kushnikov-863][^cite_note-864] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 9 November | Korsar | A [*Korsar*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luch_Korsar) drone was recorded crashed in Ukraine.[^cite_note-865] |
| **Total** | **9** | 3 Forpost, 1 Korsar, 1 Mohajer-6, 4 Orion |  |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 11 January | Forpost | A Forpost hit electric power lines and crashed near [Severny, Belgorod Oblast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Severny,_Belgorod_Oblast).[^cite_note-866] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 15 May | Orion - I | The remains of an Orion drone were recorded in Kherson.[^cite_note-867] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 6 June | Mohajer-6 | The remains of a Mohajer-6 drone, supplied by Iran, were recorded in eastern Crimea. The drone was jammed by friendly EW warfare, according to Russian sources.[^cite_note-868] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 31 July | Forpost | A Forpost drone crashed in [Taganrog](1288065.md), Rostov Oblast.[^cite_note-869] |
| **Total** | **4** | 2 Forpost, 1 Mohajer-6, 1 Orion |  |

| [Russia](25391.md) | 24 September | Orion - I | An Orion drone crashed into industrial area in Taganrog, Rostov Oblast during landing at [Taganrog-Central air base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taganrog-Central_air_base).[^cite_note-870] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 5 October | [S-70](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sukhoi_S-70_Okhotnik-B) | An S-70 Okhotnik-B UCAV, call sign *Red 074*, shot down by deliberate friendly fire from a Su-57 after losing contact with ground control and approaching the Ukrainian border. The drone crashed near [Kostiantynivka](2879020.md), Ukraine.[^cite_note-871] |
| [Russia](25391.md) | 20 October | Orion - I | An Orion drone was shot down by [9K35 Strela-10](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/9K35_Strela-10) surface-to-air missile system of the [80th Air Assault Brigade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/80th_Air_Assault_Brigade_\(Ukraine\)) in Kursk Oblast.[^cite_note-872] |

The drone managed to escape into Russian-held territory.[^cite_note-873] A Russian source claimed that the drone managed to landed safely.[^cite_note-874] |

### Other operators

| Operator | Date | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Hungary](13275.md) [Hungarian](13275.md) civilian aircraft | 27 February 2022 | [Cessna 152](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cessna_152) | A [Cessna 152](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cessna_152) with registration *HA-WAS* stored in the [Antonov An-225](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antonov_An-225_Mriya) hangar at Hostomel Airport was crushed by the left wing of the destroyed Antonov An-225.[^cite_note-878] |
| **(3)** [Azerbaijan Air Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Azerbaijani_Air_Forces) | 28 February 2022 | MiG-29 | Three MiG-29s being refitted in the Lviv State Aircraft Plant were reported destroyed or damaged on the ground by Russian strikes.[^cite_note-879][^cite_note-880][^cite_note-Lvivhangar-135] |
| **(2)** [Romanian Air Force](2106520.md) | 2 March 2022 | [MiG-21 Lancer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MiG-21#Romania), [IAR 330](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IAR_330) | A [MiG-21 Lancer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MiG-21#Romania) was lost while on an air patrol inside Romanian airspace near [Cogealac](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cogealac), 60 miles from the Ukrainian border. This "occurred amid increased air police missions in Romania after the Russian invasion of Ukraine." An [IAR 330](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IAR_330) on a search and rescue mission for the missing MiG-21 crashed with seven fatalities. The eight servicemen who died in the two accidents were posthumously promoted and decorated by the [president of Romania](273659.md).[^cite_note-881][^cite_note-882][^cite_note-883] |
| [San Marino](27248.md) [San Marino](27248.md) civilian aircraft | 10 April 2022 | [Gulfstream G150](3493147.md) | Registration *T7-DSD* was destroyed by Russian strike while stored in a hangar at [Dnipro International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dnipro_International_Airport). The G150 was registered in San Marino and owned by AC Terra International (registered in the British Virgin Islands) for businessman Vadym Vladimirovich Yermolaiev.[^cite_note-884] |
| [Belarusian Air Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Belarusian_Air_Force) | 11 July 2022 | Chekan | Ukrainian forces shot down a Belarusian Chekan loitering munition.[^cite_note-885][^cite_note-886] |
| [United States Air Force](32090.md) | 14 March 2023 | [MQ-9 Reaper](4769988.md) | A US [MQ-9 Reaper](4769988.md) UCAV was lost over the Black Sea after it [was intercepted](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Black_Sea_drone_incident) by two Russian Su-27s, one of which intentionally downed the drone by [striking](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aerial_ramming) its propeller.[^cite_note-887] |
| [Transnistria](376581.md) [Transnistria](376581.md) military aircraft | 17 March 2024 | [Mil Mi-8](957233.md) | A stored [Mil Mi-8](957233.md) helicopter of the forces of Transnistria was destroyed on the ground by a suicide drone in Tiraspol airport.[^cite_note-888] |
| [United States Air Force](32090.md) | 4 June 2024 | [Northrop Grumman RQ-4 Global Hawk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northrop_Grumman_RQ-4_Global_Hawk) | A US RQ-4 ISR drone was reported missing over the Black Sea. Russian officials declined to comment on the event.[^cite_note-889] |
| [United States](3434750.md) **(4)** [United States](3434750.md) civilian aircraft | 27 April 2025 | [PZL-110 Koliber](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PZL-110_Koliber), [Piper PA-28 Cherokee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piper_PA-28_Cherokee), [Piper PA-32 Cherokee Six](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piper_PA-32_Cherokee_Six), [Beechcraft Baron](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beechcraft_Baron) | A PZL-Okecie PZL-110 Koliber 150A *N150AQ*, Piper PA-28-235 Cherokee Pathfinder *N56937*, and a Piper PA-32 Cherokee Six *N56131*, all owned by Qualitair LLC, that were stored at a hangar in [Zhytomyr Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zhytomyr_Airport) were damaged beyond repair due to a Russian drone strike at the airport. In addition, a Beechcraft 58P Baron *N333RF* owned by the same owner was heavily damaged.[^cite_note-890][^cite_note-891][^cite_note-892][^cite_note-893] |
| [Denmark](76972.md) [Danish](76972.md) civilian aircraft | [Cessna 172](173462.md) | A Cessna 172M with registration *OY-JPC* stored at a hangar in [Zhytomyr Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zhytomyr_Airport) received minor damage due to a Russian drone strike at the airport.[^cite_note-894] |  |
